+{% block extra_js %}
+<script>
+// Add ff-input class to Django-generated fields
+document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
+    document.querySelectorAll('#login-form input[type="text"], #login-form input[type="email"], #login-form input[type="password"]').forEach(function (el) {
+        el.classList.add('ff-input');
+    });
+    // Loading state on submit
+    var form = document.getElementById('login-form');
+    var btn  = document.getElementById('login-submit-btn');
+    if (form && btn) {
+        form.addEventListener('submit', function () {
+            btn.classList.add('is-loading');
+        });
+    }
+});
+</script>
 {% endblock %}

+<script>
+// Dynamic greeting based on local time
+(function () {
+    var el = document.getElementById('dash-time-label');
+    if (!el) return;
+    var h = new Date().getHours();
+    if      (h < 12) el.textContent = 'Good morning';
+    else if (h < 17) el.textContent = 'Good afternoon';
+    else             el.textContent = 'Good evening';
+})();
+</script>

+{% block extra_js %}
+<script>
+document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
+    var input = document.getElementById('attachments-input');
+    if (input) {
+        input.addEventListener('change', function () {
+            var count = input.files ? input.files.length : 0;
+            if (count > 0) {
+                input.style.borderColor = 'var(--success)';
+                input.style.background = 'var(--success-subtle)';
+            } else {
+                input.style.borderColor = '';
+                input.style.background = '';
+            }
+        });
+        input.addEventListener('dragover', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); input.style.borderColor = 'var(--accent)'; });
+        input.addEventListener('dragleave', function () { input.style.borderColor = ''; });
+        input.addEventListener('drop', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); input.style.borderColor = 'var(--success)'; });
+    }
+});

+  /* =========================================================
+     Scroll-reveal (data-reveal elements)
+     ========================================================= */
+  function initReveal() {
+    var elements = document.querySelectorAll("[data-reveal]");
+    if (!elements.length) return;
+
+    if ("IntersectionObserver" in window) {
+      var observer = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
+        entries.forEach(function (entry) {
+          if (entry.isIntersecting) {
+            entry.target.classList.add("is-revealed");
+            observer.unobserve(entry.target);
+          }
+        });
+      }, { threshold: 0.1, rootMargin: "0px 0px -40px 0px" });
+
+      elements.forEach(function (el) { observer.observe(el); });
+    } else {
+      // Fallback: reveal all
+      elements.forEach(function (el) { el.classList.add("is-revealed"); });
+    }
+  }
+
+  /* =========================================================
+     Confirm dialogs (data-confirm attribute on links/forms)
+     ========================================================= */
+  function initConfirm() {
+    // Handle data-confirm on buttons/links
+    document.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
+      var el = e.target.closest("[data-confirm]");
+      if (!el) return;
+      var msg = el.getAttribute("data-confirm") || "Are you sure?";
+      if (!window.confirm(msg)) {
+        e.preventDefault();
+        e.stopPropagation();
+      }
+    });
+  }
+
+  /* =========================================================
+     Tooltip (data-tooltip attribute)
+     ========================================================= */
+  function initTooltips() {
+    var tip = null;
+    document.addEventListener("mouseover", function (e) {
+      var el = e.target.closest("[data-tooltip]");
+      if (!el) return;
+      var text = el.getAttribute("data-tooltip");
+      if (!text) return;
+
+      tip = document.createElement("div");
+      tip.textContent = text;
+      tip.style.cssText = [
+        "position:fixed;z-index:9999;background:var(--text-primary);color:var(--canvas);",
+        "padding:4px 10px;border-radius:6px;font-size:12px;font-weight:500;",
+        "pointer-events:none;white-space:nowrap;font-family:var(--font-sans);",
+        "box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);",
+        "opacity:0;transition:opacity 150ms;",
+      ].join("");
+      document.body.appendChild(tip);
+
+      var rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
+      tip.style.left = (rect.left + rect.width / 2 - tip.offsetWidth / 2) + "px";
+      tip.style.top = (rect.top - tip.offsetHeight - 6) + "px";
+      requestAnimationFrame(function () { tip.style.opacity = "1"; });
+    });
+
+    document.addEventListener("mouseout", function (e) {
+      var el = e.target.closest("[data-tooltip]");
+      if (!el || !tip) return;
+      tip.remove(); tip = null;
+    });
+  }
+
+  /* =========================================================
+     Avatar dropdown (icon sync + dropdown)
+     ========================================================= */
+  function initAvatarDropdown() {
+    var btn = document.getElementById("avatar-btn");
+    var drop = document.getElementById("avatar-dropdown");
+    if (!btn || !drop) return;
+
+    function openDrop() {
+      drop.style.opacity = "1";
+      drop.style.transform = "translateY(0) scale(1)";
+      drop.style.pointerEvents = "auto";
+      btn.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "true");
+    }
+    function closeDrop() {
+      drop.style.opacity = "0";
+      drop.style.transform = "translateY(-6px) scale(0.97)";
+      drop.style.pointerEvents = "none";
+      btn.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
+    }
+
+    btn.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
+      e.stopPropagation();
+      var open = drop.style.pointerEvents === "auto";
+      open ? closeDrop() : openDrop();
+    });
+    document.addEventListener("click", function () { closeDrop(); });
+    drop.addEventListener("click", function (e) { e.stopPropagation(); });
+
+    // Keyboard navigation in dropdown
+    btn.addEventListener("keydown", function (e) {
+      if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") {
+        e.preventDefault();
+        var open = drop.style.pointerEvents === "auto";
+        open ? closeDrop() : openDrop();
+      }
+      if (e.key === "Escape") closeDrop();
+    });
+  }
+
+  /* =========================================================

+      themeToggle.addEventListener("click", function () {

+      });
+    }
+  }

+          e.preventDefault();
+          e.stopPropagation();
+        }
+      });
+    });
+  }

+<script>
+(function(){
+    var icon = document.getElementById('pub-theme-icon');
+    function syncIcon(){
+        if (!icon) return;
+        icon.className = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark' ? 'bi bi-sun' : 'bi bi-moon-stars';
+    }
+    syncIcon();
+    var btn = document.getElementById('pub-theme-btn');
+    if (btn) btn.addEventListener('click', function(){ setTimeout(syncIcon, 50); });
+})();
